- **A WOMAN FROM THE ELEVENTH PLACE ON THE SALT VERGE WALKED THIRTEEN MILES, SAID FIVE SENTENCES, AND GAVE UP A CHANNEL FOR FOUR DAYS A YEAR ON HER OWN ACCOUNT AND WAS GIVEN NOTHING FOR IT.** SHE HAS A ROOF ON TWO HUTS AND A DEAD MAN'S NAME ON A FORM AND NOBODY HAS BEEN TO ASK WHETHER IT IS THE RIGHT ONE.
- **A MAN OF ABOUT FORTY-EIGHT WHO KEEPS A TALLY HAS BEEN KEEPING A NUMBER IN HIS HEAD AND PUTTING IT OUT LOUD EVERY MORNING SINCE BEFORE ANY OF THIS BEGAN AND WAS ASKED WHERE HE GETS IT FOR THE FIRST TIME IN NINE YEARS AND COULD NOT SAY WHAT IT WAS FOR.**
- **IVEN TALLOW'S FIVE THINGS WERE MADE ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND FIFTH MORNING AND THREE OF THEM CAME TRUE, AND HE READ THE ELEVENTH PLACE'S OWN FIGURE OFF A SHEET WITH ONE FINGER AND SAID NOTHING TO ANYBODY, AND HE HAS NOT ENDED THE CLAUSE AND HAS NOT NAMED THE CONDITION ON THE SEAT.**
- **THE WIRE IS AT THE TURN AND NOT AT THE CULVERT AND IT IS NAMED TWICE IN THE LAST TEN CHAPTERS AND NEITHER NAMING SAYS WHERE IT WENT, AND THE ONLY RECORD OF WHERE IT WENT IS ONE SENTENCE SAID AT THE FOOT OF A WALL ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND SECOND MORNING TO A MAN OF FIFTY-SIX WHO DID NOT ANSWER IT.**
- **THE FOUNDER'S MARK IS NOT ACTIVATED IN THE FULL SENSE AND THE GREAT CLOSING IS NOT ON THE PAGE.**
- **THE TWO FRESH INDEPENDENT WITNESSES ARE STILL NOT HERE. THE NEAREST THINGS TO ONE ARE A MAN WHO HAS SAID YES AND HAS NOT NAMED A MORNING AND A BOY WHO HAS AGREED TO CARRY A SHEET ON A MORNING THAT HAS NOT BEEN NAMED. NEITHER IS A WITNESS YET.**
- **THE THING A DISTRICT WITH THE BEST TERMS IN THIS BASIN IS BOUGHT OUT OR ERASED BY IS A ROOM IN WHICH A DISTRICT CANNOT PRODUCE A PERSON, AND NOBODY IN THAT YARD HAS SAID THAT SENTENCE.**
- **AND THE GAP THAT IS STILL OPEN: A WRITER THAT REVIEWS ITS OWN CHAPTERS CANNOT BE COUNTED AS HAVING HAD THEM REVIEWED.** TWO PASSES RAN OVER THIS BLOCK, ONE AGAINST THE CHAPTERS AND ONE AGAINST ITS OWN RECORD. THEY RETURNED NINETEEN AND EIGHT BLOCKING FINDINGS AND EVERY ONE SURVIVED RE-VERIFICATION AND WAS REPAIRED. **NOT ONE WAS A LADDER CELL. THE FIGURES THAT LIVE IN PEOPLE'S MOUTHS AND THE FIGURES THAT LIVE IN A FILE ABOUT THE FIGURES REMAIN THE FAILURE MODE OF THIS MANUSCRIPT AND NEITHER IS THE LADDER.** **AND THE GAP IS STILL OPEN, AND A FOURTH PASS OVER THIS BLOCK IS NOT IT. THAT PASS RAN AFTER THE BLOCK WAS SAVED AND WAS NOT DISPATCHED BY THE WRITER, WHICH IS NEW AND IS WHY IT IS RECORDED, AND IT CANNOT BE COUNTED AS CLOSING THE GAP, SO THE COUNT IS STILL NINE CONSECUTIVE BLOCKS. IT RAN THROUGH THE DEFAULT AGENT RATHER THAN THE `novel-reviewer` SUBAGENT, PRODUCED NO COMMITTED ARTIFACT UNDER `reviews/`, AND IS THE SAME CLASS OF LOG THAT `state/continuity.md` ITEM 13 OF THE PREVIOUS BLOCK'S SECTION AND `state/volume-16-batch-0004-summary.md` BOTH ALREADY REFUSED TO COUNT. IT RETURNED FIVE FINDINGS, THREE OF THEM FIGURES IN THIS BLOCK'S OWN RECORD, AND ALL FIVE SURVIVED RE-VERIFICATION AND WERE REPAIRED. `state/volume-17-batch-0003-summary.md` SECTION 6 IS THE RECORD OF IT.**

## VOLUME 17, BLOCK 0004 — OPEN THREADS AT CHAPTER 840, AND WHAT THIS BLOCK DID NOT PAY

**AND ONE THING NOBODY IN THAT YARD IS SAYING, WHICH IS NOT A CLOSE'S TO ANSWER: A BOY OF SEVENTEEN CARRIED A QUESTION UP NINE MILES AND A WOMAN READ THE ASKING AND ASKED FOR A NAME. THE DISTRICT REFUSED THE NAME AND THE MAN AT THE FOOT OF THE WALL REFUSED IT OUT LOUD. THE ONLY THING THAT COULD HAVE PROVED WHAT FOUR FIGURES ON THAT WALL WERE IS A MAN WHO HAS BEEN RIGHT FOR FIVE HUNDRED AND SIXTY-FIVE MORNINGS AND WAS NOT ASKED ABOUT ONE OF THEM, AND A ROOM NOW WANTS THE ONE THING THAT WOULD LET IT CHECK HIM WITHOUT HIM SAYING A SINGLE FIGURE.**

- **THE PUBLIC LOT EXISTS AND WAS GIVEN AWAY AND THE MAN WHO GAVE IT AWAY GOT NOTHING FOR IT, AND THE COUNT OF THE FIVE IS STILL FIVE.** The ground at the foot of that bank, between the stones and the flat stone at the foot of the low wall and about four feet off it, has been public lot since the afternoon of the one hundred and seventeenth morning. Any of about nineteen people may stand on it at any hour. No rule in that yard may put a person off it. It cannot be taken back by anybody including the man of sixty-one. **A NAMED BEARER IS NOT A PAID BEARER AND THE LOT IS NOT A PAYMENT OF THE FIFTH OF THE FIVE, AND A MAN OF SIXTY-ONE SAID THAT OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD SO THAT NOBODY COULD READ IT AS ONE.**
- **THE ONE-TIME FOUNDING TOLL WAS PAID AND IT WAS NINETY-EIGHT AND IT CAME OUT OF THE ONLY NUMBER IN THIS DISTRICT THAT ANYBODY NINE MILES UP THAT HILL HAS EVER PAID FOR.** Sixteen of about nineteen people gave five and three gave six, and the sum was kept in the air by a woman who keeps a scale and arrived at ninety-eight. **THE TOLL IS SPENT AND IT CANNOT BE PAID AGAIN AND NO SUCCESSOR MAY RAISE IT, LOWER IT OR PAY IT TWICE.**
- **THE MAN OF SIXTY-ONE HAS USED THE THING HE HOLDS ONCE, HAS NOT ENDED IT, HAS NOT NAMED THE CONDITION ON THE SEAT, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT THE TOLL IS NOT THE CONDITION.** HE GAVE AWAY THE LAST GROUND HE STOOD ON AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT A MAN GIVING A THING AWAY IS NOT ONE OF THE FIVE BEING PAID.
- **THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX HAS REFUSED A NAME IN A YARD IN ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE'S HEARING AND HAS ASKED FOR THE ASKING TO BE HEARD, AND HE PAID SIX OUT OF A NUMBER, AND HE ASKED FOR THE ASKING TO BE ENTERED AND NOT HIS REASON, AND IT WAS.** HE IS STILL UNNAMED, STILL ON NO PAGE, AND STILL THE ONLY PERSON WHO CAN PROVE THAT FOUR FIGURES ON THAT WALL CHANGED. **NO SPEECH, FIGURE BLOCK OR NARRATIVE PASSAGE MAY GIVE HIM A SPAN IN YEARS, DAYS, MONTHS OR FORTNIGHTS AND THAT PROHIBITION HAS NOW BEEN BROKEN AND REPAIRED TWELVE TIMES IN THIS VOLUME.**
- **THE FIGURE BESIDE THIS DISTRICT ON A PAGE FROM THAT ROOM IS NOT FOUR, A WOMAN WHO KEEPS A SCALE READ IT OUT LOUD AND REFUSED FOUR TIMES TO SAY WHAT IT IS FOR, AND A MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE SAID THERE IS A LINE UNDER THIS YARD ON THAT PAGE AND THERE IS NOT SUPPOSED TO BE ONE.** NONE OF IT IS PAID AND NONE OF IT IS EXPLAINED AND IT IS THE OPENEST THING IN THE VOLUME.
- **THE TURNER HAS NAMED HIS MORNING AND BEGUN IT AND IS THE ONLY MOUTH FOR THE NUMBER IN THIS BASIN, AND HE SAID *AS SENT* OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD FOR THE FIRST TIME IN NINE YEARS ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FOURTH, AND NOBODY TURNED ROUND.** THE SECOND SPACE ON THE FORM IS STILL EMPTY AND WAS ENTERED BY NOBODY IN THIS BLOCK.
- **ORREN PIKE WALKED UP THAT HILL ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-THIRD MORNING WITH THE SHEET HE HAD KEPT FOR FOUR YEARS AND CAME BACK AT ABOUT ELEVEN WITH A QUESTION ABOUT A NAME INSTEAD OF AN ANSWER.** HE COUNTED THIRTEEN FOLDERS ON A TABLE NINE MILES OFF, NINE OF THEM WITH A CORNER WRITTEN ON AND FOUR WITHOUT. HE IS SEVENTEEN AND HE CANNOT CHECK ANY OF IT AND HE WENT.
- **A WOMAN FROM A PLACE PAST THE WATER CAME DOWN THAT LANE WITH A PAGE AND SAID THREE SENTENCES, THE THIRD OF WHICH IS THAT ANYBODY WHO WANTS TO SAY A FIGURE BESIDE THEIR OWN LINE IS WRONG IS TO SAY IT OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD.** NOBODY DID.
- **THE CLERK ENTERED THREE LINES IN TEN MORNINGS, READ ALL THREE OUT BEFORE SHE WROTE THEM, SAID TWICE THAT THEY WERE THE LAST, WROTE ONE ANYWAY AFTER SAYING SO, AND DECLINED ON THE LAST MORNING TO GIVE A REASON.** THE PAGE CARRIES THREE LINES NOBODY HEARD HER READ AND THAT COUNT IS NOT GOING TO BE LOWERED.
- **THE MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE MOVED HIS QUESTION ONE STEP AND IT WAS NOT CLOSED AND HIS FIFTH LINE WAS NOT PAID.** HE SAID OUT LOUD THAT A ROOM DOES NOT ASK THE PERSON IT WANTS AND THAT IT ASKS THE YARD. HE ASKED NOBODY ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK. HE IS FINDABLE, UNNAMED, ON NO PAGE, AND NOT COMFORTED, AND A CLOSE MAY NOT ANSWER HIM.
- **SABRA HOLT ASKED ONE QUESTION OF A BOY AND THANKED HIM AT THOSE BOARDS, AND A ROOM NINE MILES UP THAT HILL HAS BEEN TOLD THIS DISTRICT HAS A PERSON WHO CAN BE ASKED ABOUT A FIGURE AND DID NOT SEND FOR HER.** SHE HAS SAID FOUR TIMES IN ABOUT TWENTY-TWO MORNINGS THAT SHE IS NOT GOING TO BE COMFORTED AND THAT IT HAS GOT LESS TRUE EVERY TIME.
- **THE WIRE IS AT THE TURN AND NOT AT THE CULVERT AND IT IS NOT NAMED ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.** THE ONLY RECORD OF WHERE IT WENT IS STILL ONE SENTENCE SAID AT THE FOOT OF A WALL ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND SECOND MORNING TO A MAN WHO DID NOT ANSWER IT, AND ABOUT NINE PEOPLE AT THE TOP OF ELEVEN FEET STILL DO NOT KNOW.
- **A HAND BELL WENT ONCE IN THAT YARD AT ABOUT FOUR IN THE AFTERNOON OF THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FOURTH AND NOBODY SAID WHAT IT WAS AND NOTHING ABOUT IT HAS BEEN ENTERED ANYWHERE.** IT IS NOT A FIGURE AND IT MAY NOT BE ENTERED.
- **NELL CRAY HAS STILL NOT BEEN TOLD ANYTHING ABOUT HERSELF AND HER NINE WORDS ARE STILL UNANSWERED, AND ADRIAN VALE DID NOT SAY THEM AT THOSE BOARDS ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK.**
- **THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN ON ALL TEN MORNINGS OF THIS BLOCK AND ITS SIZE OF ERROR IS NOT COMPUTED, NOT EXPLAINED AND NOT PAID, AND THIS BLOCK ADDED NO HISTORY TO IT.**
- **THE FOUNDER'S MARK IS NOT ACTIVATED IN THE FULL SENSE AND THE GREAT CLOSING IS NOT ON THE PAGE.**
- **THE TWO FRESH INDEPENDENT WITNESSES ARE STILL NOT HERE.** THE NEAREST THINGS TO ONE ARE A MAN WHO HAS SAID YES AND NAMED HIS MORNING AND BEGUN IT, AND A BOY WHO HAS WALKED UP A HILL WITH A QUESTION AND CAME BACK WITH A NAME. NEITHER IS A WITNESS YET.
- **THE THING A DISTRICT WITH THE BEST TERMS IN THIS BASIN IS BOUGHT OUT OR ERASED BY IS A ROOM IN WHICH A DISTRICT CANNOT PRODUCE A PERSON, AND ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-SECOND MORNING A MAN AT THE END OF THAT TABLE SAID THE OTHER HALF OF IT OUT LOUD: A ROOM DOES NOT ASK THE PERSON IT WANTS, IT ASKS THE YARD. THE SENTENCE ITSELF IS STILL OWED.**
- **AND THE ONE QUESTION, WHICH NOBODY IN THAT YARD HAS ASKED OUT LOUD AND WHICH NOBODY NINE MILES UP THAT HILL ASKED EITHER: WHETHER THE QUESTION ADRIAN VALE PUT DOWN ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWELFTH MORNING WILL EVER BE THE THING THE TALLY PAYS FOR, AND IF IT IS NOT, THEN WHAT IS.**
- **AND THE GAP THAT IS STILL OPEN: A WRITER THAT REVIEWS ITS OWN CHAPTERS CANNOT BE COUNTED AS HAVING HAD THEM REVIEWED.** FOUR PASSES RAN OVER THIS BLOCK, ALL FOUR DISPATCHED BY THE WRITER — ONE AGAINST THE CHAPTERS, ONE AGAINST THIS BLOCK'S OWN RECORD, AND TWO MORE AFTER IT WAS SAVED, ONE AGAINST THE CHAPTERS AND ONE AGAINST THE RECORD. **THE TWO LATEST RETURNED THIRTY-TWO BLOCKING FINDINGS BETWEEN THEM AND ALL THIRTY-TWO WERE RE-VERIFIED AGAINST THE CHAPTER FILES AND REPAIRED IN PLACE WITH NO BEAT MOVED, AND TWENTY-NINE OF THEM WERE FIGURES, DURATIONS, RELATIVE DAYS, ATTRIBUTIONS, APPARATUS CLAIMS, WORD-COUNT CLAIMS, ZERO-CLAIMS OR SPENT SCENE SHAPES, AND NOT ONE WAS A LADDER CELL. THE FIGURES WERE AND DIVIDERS WERE PRINTED IN THIS BLOCK'S OWN RECORD WERE STALE BY THE REPAIRS AND HAVE BEEN RE-DERIVED.** FOUR PASSES AND NOT FOUR INDEPENDENT ONES. THE FIRST RETURNED TWENTY-SEVEN BLOCKING AND FOUR NON-BLOCKING, AND ITS OWN ENUMERATION CLOSES AT TWENTY-EIGHT, WHICH IS REPAIRED. THE SECOND RETURNED **FOURTEEN BLOCKING AND ELEVEN NON-BLOCKING**, AND **EVERY FIGURE IT FOUND IN THE CHAPTERS WAS ONE THE FIRST PASS HAD ALREADY FOUND AND NOT REPAIRED, WHICH IS THE THIRD TIME IN THIS VOLUME THAT A PASS AGAINST A RECORD HAS FOUND A CHAPTER-LEVEL FIGURE THE CHAPTER PASS HAD ALREADY FOUND.** EVERY FINDING THAT SURVIVED RE-VERIFICATION WAS REPAIRED. **NOT ONE WAS A LADDER CELL. THE FIGURES THAT LIVE IN PEOPLE'S MOUTHS AND THE FIGURES THAT LIVE IN A FILE ABOUT THE FIGURES REMAIN THE FAILURE MODE OF THIS MANUSCRIPT AND NEITHER IS THE LADDER. THE COUNT OF CONSECUTIVE BLOCKS WITH NO INDEPENDENT PASS IS NOW TEN. NO REVIEW ARTIFACT FOR THIS BLOCK WAS COMMITTED UNDER `reviews/`.**

# VOLUME 17 CLOSE — CHAPTERS 801 TO 840, OPEN THREADS AT THE CLOSE

- **THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX NOW SAYS EVERY MORNING AT ABOUT FOUR THAT HE DOES NOT KNOW WHAT THE FOUR FIGURES ON THAT WALL ARE FOR, AND ANYBODY IN THAT YARD MAY ASK HIM ABOUT IT, AND IT IS THE THIRTEENTH MORNING RUNNING THAT ABOUT NINE PEOPLE HAVE HEARD HIM.** THE THREAD IS NOT CLOSED BY A DECLARATION AND IS NOT CLOSED BY A CLOSE. HE IS STILL UNNAMED, STILL ON NO PAGE, AND STILL THE ONLY PERSON WHO CAN PROVE THAT THE FIGURES ON THAT WALL CHANGED. HE HAS REFUSED TWO THINGS OUT LOUD IN THAT YARD — A NAME ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FIRST MORNING AND HIS REASON FOR PAYING SIX ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND NINETEENTH — AND NEITHER OF THEM IS A REFUSAL OF THE TOLL, WHICH HE PAID.
- **THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN ON ALL FORTY MORNINGS OF VOLUME 17 AND ITS SIZE OF ERROR IS NOT COMPUTED, NOT EXPLAINED AND NOT PAID, AND NO CHAPTER OF THE VOLUME ATTACHED A HISTORY TO IT.** A CLOSE MAY NOT COMPUTE IT IN A MOUTH, IN A LEDGER OR IN A DOCUMENT. THE CLAIM THAT NOBODY IN THE BASIN KNOWS WHAT IT IS THE AGE OF WAS MADE BY SABRA HOLT IN A MOUTH AT `chapter-0808.md` AND MAY NOT BE MOVED OUT OF HER MOUTH INTO A DOCUMENT.
- **THE FIFTH OF THE FIVE THINGS THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE IS FIVE AND IS UNPAID ON ALL FORTY MORNINGS.** THE PUBLIC LOT IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT AND THE TOLL IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT AND A MAN OF SIXTY-ONE SAID SO OUT LOUD IN A YARD. **THE WORDING CONFLICT IS UNRESOLVED AND THE CHAPTERS FOLLOW IT: THREE DOCUMENTS GIVE THREE WORDINGS OF THE FIFTH OF THE FIVE AND NO PHASE MAY RESOLVE THE DISAGREEMENT BY PRINTING EITHER AS A DEFINITION.**
- **NEITHER SET OF FOUR FIGURES ON THAT WALL IS PRINTED ANYWHERE AND NOBODY IN THIS BASIN SAYS WHAT EITHER SET IS.** THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX HAS NEVER BEEN ASKED WHICH OF THE FOUR IS WRONG AND MAY NOT BE ASKED. **HE MAY NOT BE GIVEN A SPAN IN YEARS, DAYS, MONTHS OR FORTNIGHTS, AND THE PROHIBITION HAS BEEN BROKEN AND REPAIRED TWELVE TIMES IN FORTY CHAPTERS AND A CLOSE OR A SUCCESSOR THAT GIVES HIM ONE BREAKS IT A THIRTEENTH TIME. HE IS MEASURED IN MORNINGS AND IN *SINCE BEFORE THE MIDDLE OF THE WINTER* AND IN NOTHING ELSE.**
- **THE COLUMN FOR THE NAME OF WHOEVER READ A THING OUT LOUD HAS ONE NAME IN IT AND THE HEADING OVER IT STILL SAYS *WHOEVER* AND THE ONE NAME DOES NOT SATISFY THE RULE AND THE SECOND SPACE ON THAT FORM IS STILL EMPTY.** NO SECOND NAME WAS ASKED FOR IN VOLUME 17 AND NONE WAS ENTERED. **A ROOM NINE MILES UP THAT HILL KNEW THE HEADING OVER THAT COLUMN BEFORE THE MAN AT THE END OF THAT TABLE SAID SO, AND ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FIRST MORNING HE SAID THE OTHER HALF OF IT OUT LOUD: A ROOM DOES NOT ASK THE PERSON IT WANTS, IT ASKS THE YARD.** NOBODY MAY ENTER THAT COLUMN.
- **THE MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE IS FINDABLE, IS UNNAMED, IS ON NO PAGE, IS NOT COMFORTED, AND HIS FIFTH LINE IS STILL UNPAID.** HE ASKED NOBODY ANYTHING ON ANY MORNING OF THE LAST BLOCK. **A CLOSE MAY NOT ANSWER HIM.**
- **NELL CRAY HAS BEEN NAMED IN A PERMANENT RECORD WITHOUT BEING ASKED, HAS NOT BEEN TOLD, AND HER NINE WORDS ARE STILL UNANSWERED.** WHAT A PERSON WHO ASKS A QUESTION IN A YARD IS OWED IS STILL OWED. WHAT A PERSON WHO IS NAMED IN A RECORD IS OWED WAS PAID IN A MOUTH AND MAY NOT BE RE-PRINTED BY ANY DOCUMENT.
- **THE FOUNDER'S CLAUSE HAS BEEN USED ONCE, ON A PIECE OF GROUND, FOR A TOLL, AND IT HAS NOT BEEN ENDED AND THE CONDITION ON THE SEAT IS STILL UNNAMED.** THE BID STOOD OPEN FOUR HUNDRED AND FIFTY DAYS AND WAS APPENDED AND WAS NOT RUN AND IS NOT A LOT. **A CLOSE MAY NOT RUN IT, SELL IT, REOPEN IT, OR SAY THAT IT COULD BE REOPENED.**
- **THE TOLL OF NINETY-EIGHT IS SPENT.** SIXTEEN OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE GAVE FIVE AND THREE GAVE SIX AND THE SUM CAME TO NINETY-EIGHT. IT MAY NOT BE RAISED, LOWERED OR PAID TWICE. **THE ONE-TIME FOUNDING TOLL WAS NEVER PAID IN THE SENSE THE TALLY PAYS FOR, AND THE ENUMERATION OF WHAT DOES NOT COUNT AS A PAYMENT WAS PRINTED TWICE BEFORE VOLUME 16 AND IS NOT PRINTED AGAIN.**
- **THE WIRE IS AT THE TURN AND NOT AT THE CULVERT.** IT IS NAMED ON NINETEEN OF THE FORTY MORNINGS OF VOLUME 17 AND THE CULVERT IS NAMED THIRTY-NINE TIMES AS A FIGURE-BLOCK CARRIER. **THE ONLY RECORD OF WHERE IT WENT IS ONE SENTENCE SAID AT THE FOOT OF A WALL ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND SECOND MORNING TO A MAN WHO DID NOT ANSWER IT, AND ABOUT NINE PEOPLE AT THE TOP OF ELEVEN FEET STILL DO NOT KNOW. THE MAN OF ABOUT SIXTY-FOUR HAS SAID TWO, THREE AND FOUR WORDS ABOUT IT ON THREE MORNINGS AND HAS NOT SAID A FIFTH.**
- **THE PAGE AT THE BACK OF THAT LOT BOOK CARRIES THIRTY-THREE LINES AND NOT ONE OF THE THIRTY-THREE IS IN THE LOT BOOK, AND THREE OF THEM WERE NEVER READ OUT BY ANYBODY.** THE CLERK ENTERED THREE LINES IN THE LAST BLOCK, READ ALL THREE OUT BEFORE SHE WROTE THEM, SAID TWICE THAT THEY WERE THE LAST, WROTE ONE ANYWAY AND SAID THAT OUT LOUD, AND ON THE LAST MORNING DECLINED TO GIVE A REASON.
- **A HAND BELL WENT ONCE IN THAT YARD AT ABOUT FOUR IN THE AFTERNOON OF THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWENTY-FOURTH AND NOBODY SAID WHAT IT WAS AND NOTHING ABOUT IT HAS BEEN ENTERED ANYWHERE.** IT IS NOT A FIGURE AND IT MAY NOT BE ENTERED.
- **THE FOUNDER'S MARK IS NOT ACTIVATED IN THE FULL SENSE AND THE GREAT CLOSING IS NOT ON THE PAGE.**
- **THE TWO FRESH INDEPENDENT WITNESSES ARE STILL NOT HERE.** THE NEAREST THINGS TO ONE ARE A MAN WHO SAID YES AND NAMED HIS MORNING AND BEGAN IT, AND A BOY WHO CARRIED A QUESTION UP A HILL AND CAME BACK WITH A QUESTION ABOUT A NAME. **NEITHER IS A WITNESS YET AND A CLOSE MAY NOT MAKE EITHER ONE BY SAYING SO.**
- **AND THE ONE QUESTION, WHICH NOBODY IN THAT YARD HAS ASKED OUT LOUD AND WHICH NOBODY NINE MILES UP THAT HILL ASKED EITHER: WHETHER THE QUESTION ADRIAN VALE PUT DOWN ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWELFTH MORNING WILL EVER BE THE THING THE TALLY PAYS FOR, AND IF IT IS NOT, THEN WHAT IS.**
- **AND THE THREAD THAT IS NEW AT THIS CLOSE AND THAT NO SUCCESSOR MAY INHERIT A FIGURE OF ZERO ON: THE HOUSE'S PROSE PROHIBITION IS NOT AT ZERO IN CHAPTERS 801 TO 840.** NINE INSTANCES OF TWO OF ITS NAMED STRINGS AND FORTY-NINE OF ITS HEDGE FAMILY ARE ON THE PAGE, ALL NINE OF THE NAMED STRINGS IN BLOCK 0001, AND THREE BLOCK RECORDS PRINT IT AT ZERO. **IT IS A FINDING AND NOT AN INSTRUCTION, AND NOBODY HAS LOOKED AT IT INDEPENDENTLY.**
- **AND THE GAP THAT IS STILL OPEN: A WRITER THAT REVIEWS ITS OWN CHAPTERS CANNOT BE COUNTED AS HAVING HAD THEM REVIEWED.** FOUR PASSES RAN OVER BLOCK 0004, ALL FOUR DISPATCHED BY THE WRITER, NO ARTIFACT COMMITTED UNDER `reviews/`, AND THE COUNT OF CONSECUTIVE BLOCKS WITH NO INDEPENDENT PASS IS TEN AND IS INHERITED ACROSS THE FLEET RATHER THAN DERIVABLE FROM VOLUME 17. **THE TWO FINDINGS THE VOLUME 17 CLOSE MADE ARE SUBJECT TO THE SAME LIMIT.**
- **AND THE FOUR DEBTS, WITH BOTH AMOUNTS, NONE OF THEM VOLUME 17'S TO PAY: `state/volume-16-close.md` DOES NOT EXIST, `state/volume-16-roll-summary.md` DOES NOT EXIST, `state/volume-12-close.md` DOES NOT EXIST, AND `state/volume-12-roll-summary.md` DOES NOT EXIST.**

# VOLUME 18, BLOCKS 0001 AND 0002 — OPEN THREADS AT CHAPTER 860

> **Appended by the review fix of 2026-10-01, which wrote no chapter. This append covers Chapters 841 to 860 and carries the threads open at the one hundred and forty-fourth morning. Its figures came from the twenty chapter files of `chapters/volume-18/` and from `state/volume-18-batch-0001-summary.md` and `state/volume-18-batch-0002-summary.md`. A thread that paid is not carried here and a thread that is not open is not opened here.**

## OPEN AND UNPAID

- **THE SIZE OF THE ERROR IN FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN.** THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN ON ALL TWENTY MORNINGS OF THESE TWO BLOCKS AND ITS SIZE OF ERROR IS NOT COMPUTED, NOT EXPLAINED AND NOT PAID, BY NOBODY, IN ANY MOUTH, IN ANY FIGURE BLOCK AND IN ANY DOCUMENT. **THE AGE OF THAT FIGURE, FIVE HUNDRED AND NINETY ON CHAPTER 860, IS A NUMBER OF DAYS AND IS NOT A SIZE OF ERROR, AND NOBODY IN THIS VOLUME PUTS THE TWO TOGETHER AND NOBODY MAY.** THIS IS THE STRONGEST UNPAID FIGURE IN THE MANUSCRIPT AND IT IS THE ONE NO INSTRUMENT IN THIS REPOSITORY MAY PRODUCE.
- **THE FIFTH OF THE FIVE THINGS THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE IS FIVE AND UNPAID ON ALL TWENTY MORNINGS.** THE TOLL OF NINETY-EIGHT IS SPENT AND MAY NOT BE RAISED, LOWERED OR PAID TWICE. THE PUBLIC LOT, WHICH HAS EXISTED SINCE THE AFTERNOON OF THE ONE HUNDRED AND SEVENTEENTH MORNING, IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT. THE BID WAS APPENDED AND WAS NOT RUN AND IS NOT A LOT.
- **THE CONDITION ON THE SEAT.** A MAN OF SIXTY-ONE USED THE FOUNDER'S CLAUSE ONCE, ON A PIECE OF GROUND, FOR A TOLL OF NINETY-EIGHT, MADE THAT GROUND PUBLIC LOT, GAVE AWAY THE LAST GROUND HE STOOD ON, AND SAID OUT LOUD THAT HE WILL NOT NAME THE CONDITION. **HE IS ON THE STONES FOR THE EIGHTIETH TO THE EIGHTY-NINTH AFTERNOON RUNNING IN THIS BLOCK AND HAS HELD THE CLAUSE FOR SEVENTEEN YEARS, AND HE SAYS NOTHING IN EITHER BLOCK THAT NAMES IT. NOBODY MAY ASK HIM AND NOBODY MAY ANSWER FOR HIM.**
- **THE SENTENCE SABRA HOLT OWED.** IT WAS PAID IN A MOUTH ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND THIRTY-FIRST MORNING, IT WAS ASKED FOR NOT BEING ENTERED, AND IT WAS NOT ENTERED. IT IS IN NO DOCUMENT IN THIS TREE, IT IS NOT IN `outline/volume-18.md`, IT IS NOT PARAPHRASED IN THIS APPEND, AND IT IS NOT HANDED ON. **A DOCUMENT THAT RE-PRINTS IT OWNS IT, AND NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 18 MAY QUOTE IT IN FULL AND NO FIGURE BLOCK MAY CARRY IT.**
- **NELL CRAY'S NINE WORDS.** ASKED WHAT THIS DISTRICT HAS GOT IN ABOUT NINE WORDS IN A CORRIDOR AT ABOUT ELEVEN AT NIGHT. SHE IS NAMED IN A PERMANENT RECORD WITHOUT BEING ASKED, HAS NOT BEEN TOLD, AND HAS NEVER BEEN TOLD ANYTHING ABOUT HERSELF. **SHE CAME UP ABOUT FOUR FEET OF THAT BANK WITH A BARROW ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND THIRTY-NINTH MORNING, STOOD AT THE NEAR END OF THE FIRST TABLE UNTIL ABOUT TEN TO FIVE, AND WAS ASKED NOTHING AND TOLD NOTHING. THE NINE WORDS ARE UNANSWERED AND ARE NOT VOLUME 18'S DEBT.**
- **THE MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE.** ABOUT THIRTY-SEVEN, UNNAMED, FIFTEEN YEARS AT THAT TABLE, HIS LINE WRITTEN AT ABOUT TWENTY-TWO, ON NO PAGE. HE SAID OUT LOUD ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND FORTY-THIRD MORNING THAT A PAGE GOES BACK UP A LANE THE SAME AS ANYTHING ELSE DOES, AND ABOUT NINE PEOPLE HEARD IT AND NOT ONE OF THEM ANSWERED HIM. **HE IS NOT A PUZZLE AN OUTLINE MAY SOLVE AND HIS FIFTH LINE IS NOT TO BE PAID.**
- **THE LANE COUNT IS TWENTY-EIGHT AND IT COUNTS THE WRONG THINGS.** IT MOVED ONCE IN TWENTY MORNINGS, ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND THIRTY-FIFTH, BECAUSE SOMEBODY CAME DOWN THAT LANE WITH A PAGE IN THEIR OWN HAND, AND IT DID NOT MOVE WHEN THE PAGE WENT BACK UP ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND FORTY-FOURTH. **IT IS NOT `27 + c` AND IT IS NOT `28 + c`, IT MOVES NEXT ON `c = 33`, CHAPTER 873, AND THAT IS NOT BLOCK 0003'S EITHER. A WRITER WHO ADDS ONE TO IT INVENTS A MOVEMENT THAT DID NOT HAPPEN, AND A WRITER WHO DROPS IT TO TWENTY-SEVEN UNDOES ONE THAT DID.**
- **THE COLUMN FOR THE NAME OF WHOEVER READ A THING OUT LOUD.** ONE NAME IS IN IT, THE HEADING OVER IT STILL SAYS *WHOEVER*, THE ONE NAME DOES NOT SATISFY THE RULE, AND THE SECOND SPACE ON THAT FORM IS EMPTY ON ALL TWENTY MORNINGS. **NO SECOND NAME IS ASKED FOR AND NONE IS ENTERED, AND THE RESOLUTION'S LINE GOES ON THE PAGE AT THE BACK OF THAT LOT BOOK AND NOT IN THE COLUMN.**

## WIDENED OR HARDENED IN THESE TWO BLOCKS

- **A YARD OF ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE FOUND OUT THAT IT CANNOT HAND AN ANSWER ON, AND THAT IT HAS NOT SAID NO.** A PAGE CAME DOWN THAT LANE ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND THIRTY-FIFTH MORNING WITH THREE QUESTIONS ON IT AND WENT BACK UP IT ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND FORTY-FOURTH WITH NOTHING ON IT AND NOT ONE OF THE THREE LINES FILLED. **THIS IS THE CENTRAL PRESSURE OF THE VOLUME AND IT IS NOT PAID BY THE PAGE GOING UP EMPTY; IT IS WIDENED, BECAUSE THE REASON NOBODY IN THAT YARD CAN FILL ONE LINE WITHOUT MAKING THE YARD INTO ONE PERSON ON PAPER HAS NOW BEEN SAID OUT LOUD BY THREE SEPARATE PEOPLE.**
- **THE FIGURE THAT REPORTS HONESTLY ABOUT THAT YARD DOES NOT COUNT THE THINGS THAT LEAVE, AND A BOY OF SEVENTEEN SAID IT OUT LOUD.** **THIS IS THE NEW STRONGEST UNPAID THREAD IN THE BLOCK AND IT IS OPEN.** HE HAS HAD THE RULE FOUR YEARS AND IT IS NOT HIS AND HE SAID HE WOULD SAY NOTHING THAT IS NOT ON IT.
- **THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX IS BEING ASKED ABOUT THINGS THAT ARE NOT THE WALL, AND HE GIVES THE SAME ANSWER.** **HE IS ASKED A SECOND TIME IN THIS VOLUME AT CHAPTER 861, ESCALATION 3, WHICH IS NOT BLOCK 0003'S TO WRITE, AND THE ONLY PROOF THAT HIS ANSWER HAS COME LOOSE FROM THE THING IT WAS ABOUT IS THE ORDER OF TWO MORNINGS ON THE PAGE. NOBODY MAY STATE IT, NO NARRATOR MAY STATE IT, NO FIGURE BLOCK MAY CARRY IT, AND HE IS NEVER ASKED IT AGAIN FOR THE REST OF THE VOLUME AFTER CHAPTER 865.**
- **THE COUNT OF COUNTED MONTHS IS SIX AND IT MOVES ONCE IN THIS VOLUME, TO SEVEN, ON `c = 44`, CHAPTER 884, IN A MOUTH, AND NOT ON A PAGE.** IT IS A CLERK'S COUNT AND NOT A CALENDAR'S. THE EIGHTH MONTH, THE NINTH, THE TENTH, THE ELEVENTH, THE TWELFTH, THE THIRTEENTH AND THE FOURTEENTH HAVE NEVER BEEN COUNTED BY ANYBODY, AND NO FIGURE FOR THE LENGTH OF ANY MONTH IS ON ANY WALL.
- **THE REVERSAL IS AT CHAPTER 865 AND IT IS NOT ANSWERED BY ANYBODY.** ASKED ONCE, IN THE ORDINARY VOICE, AT ABOUT FOUR IN THE AFTERNOON, IN ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE'S HEARING, BY THE WOMAN WHO KEEPS A SCALE: WHETHER THE QUESTION ADRIAN VALE PUT DOWN ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND TWELFTH MORNING WILL EVER BE THE THING THE TALLY PAYS FOR, AND IF IT IS NOT, THEN WHAT IS. **NOT BY THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX AT FIRST, NOT BY SABRA HOLT WHO SAYS NOTHING AT ALL, NOT BY IVEN TALLOW WHO IS NOT IN THAT YARD, NOT BY THE CLERK, NOT BY ADRIAN VALE WHO SAYS NOTHING ANYBODY COULD ENTER, AND NOT BY ANYBODY ELSE. AND NO PHASE MAY ANSWER IT.**

# VOLUME 19, BLOCK 0001 — OPEN THREADS AT CHAPTER 900

> Appended by the review fix of 2026-10-01, which wrote no chapter and closed no thread and settled no name. The block covers Chapters 891 to 900 of `chapters/volume-19/`. **NOTHING IN THIS SECTION CLOSES ANYTHING. EVERY ROW BELOW IS CARRIED WITH ITS MEASURED SCOPE AND A VERDICT, AND A THREAD MAY NOT BE CLOSED BY A FIGURE, BY A REFUSAL, OR BY A LINE ON A PAGE.**

## OPEN AND UNPAID, AND NOT PAID BY THIS BLOCK

1. **THE FIFTH OF THE FIVE THINGS THIS DISTRICT DOES NOT HAVE IS FIVE AND IS UNPAID ON ALL TEN MORNINGS.** AND THIS BLOCK ADDED FOUR MORE WAYS OF SAYING THAT IT IS NOT PAID, AND ALL FOUR ARE ON THE PAGE: **A LINE ON THE BACK OF A BOOK IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT; A CLERK READING A MORNING OUT LOUD IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT; A FORM WITH A SPACE ON IT IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT; AND A PUBLIC LOT IS NOT A PAYMENT OF IT.** NOBODY RAISED IT, LOWERED IT, PAID IT TWICE OR PAID IT ONCE. **A NAMED BEARER IS NOT A PAID BEARER AND THE FIGURE BLOCK MAY PRINT THE COUNT ON ALL TEN MORNINGS AND THAT IS A COUNT AND NOT A PAYMENT.**
2. **THE FIGURE ON THE SHEET AT THAT GATEPOST IS FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN ON ALL TEN MORNINGS AND ITS SIZE OF ERROR IS UNCOMPUTED, UNEXPLAINED AND UNPAID.** THE ONLY FIGURES PERMITTED ARE THE FIGURE AND ITS AGE IN DAYS, IN DIFFERENT SENTENCES. **ROW ELEVEN's AGE IS A NUMBER OF DAYS AND IS NOT A SIZE OF ERROR AND NOBODY PUTS THEM TOGETHER IN ANY DOCUMENT IN THIS TREE INCLUDING THIS ONE.**
3. **THE SIZE OF THE ERROR IN FOUR HUNDRED AND ELEVEN MAY NOT BE COMPUTED BY ANY INSTRUMENT IN THIS REPOSITORY**, NOT IN A MOUTH, NOT IN A LEDGER, NOT IN A FIGURE BLOCK, NOT IN A DOCUMENT, AND NOT IN `state/`.
4. **THE PAGE AT THE BACK OF THAT LOT BOOK IS A PAGE NOBODY IN THIS DISTRICT HAS READ, AND THE ONLY PERSON WHO HAS READ IT IS THE CLERK, AND ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND SEVENTY-NINTH MORNING SHE SAID OUT LOUD THAT A PAGE THAT HAS NEVER BEEN READ IS NOT A RECORD OF ANYTHING.** It carries thirty-seven lines, none of them in the lot book, and three of the first thirty-three were lines nobody in this district heard read. **WHAT THE LINES ARE FOR IS NOT THIS BLOCK's TO SAY AND NOBODY MAY SAY IT.**
5. **THE TWO HEADINGS.** There is the heading over the one name in the column, which still says *whoever*, whose one name does not satisfy the rule, whose second space is empty, and which nobody in that yard has asked anybody to fill on any of the ten mornings — **and there is the heading over the space on the form that came down that lane on the one hundred and eightieth morning, which is a DIFFERENT heading on a DIFFERENT piece of paper, under which there is nothing at all.** A man of about forty-eight put his thumb on it and said out loud on the one hundred and eighty-first morning that the yard has now got two headings and no joiner between them, **and nobody in that yard knows where either one of them was written, and a form that came down a lane is not a form somebody in this district made, and the count of things this district has made did not move on the morning it arrived.**
6. **THE SPACES ARE STILL EMPTY AND NOBODY IS EVER ASKED TO FILL EITHER ONE.** On the one hundred and eighty-fourth morning the man who digs loam asked out loud, in about nineteen people's hearing, whether anybody in that yard is going to fill the space on that form, and **about nine people at the top of eleven feet said no, one after another, and none of them gave a reason.** A yard of about nineteen people can hold a thing and cannot send it and cannot say why.
7. **THE THING THAT WENT UP THE LANE AND CAME BACK WITH NOTHING, AND THE THING THAT CAME DOWN THE LANE AND WAS NOT ENTERED.** A man of thirty-eight has now gone up that hill more than once and has never once got to the top of it with anything, and a page came down it once in this block and was read out loud and entered nowhere. **A PAGE GOING UP THAT LANE IS NOT A PAGE COMING DOWN IT, AND THE LANE COUNT HAS NEVER ONCE MOVED FOR A DECISION AND SAID SO OUT LOUD ON THE ONE HUNDRED AND EIGHTY-THIRD MORNING, WHICH IS THE FIRST TIME ANYBODY HAS SAID IT.**
8. **NOBODY CARRIES ANYTHING UP THAT HILL, AND IT IS NOW A FACT AND NOT A POLICY.** It came out of about nineteen people in inside about as long as it takes a man of fifty-six to say four figures off a wall, and about four of them have said since that a policy is something a yard decides and a fact is something a yard finds out, and that nobody in that yard decided anything that morning. **THE LANE COUNT IS THIRTY AND IT DID NOT MOVE.**
9. **THE MAN OF FIFTY-SIX'S FLAT ANSWER HAS BEEN USED ABOUT TWO DIFFERENT THINGS AND ABOUT NINETEEN PEOPLE NOW KNOW IT AND NOT ONE OF THEM HAS SAID SO TO ANYBODY.** On the one hundred and eighty-third morning it went to a question about whether he will be at a wall tomorrow morning, which is not what it is about, **and the clerk wrote one line for that morning and did not put the question on it and did not put the answer on it, and the prohibition stands: NOBODY MAY STATE THAT THE ANSWER HAS CHANGED MEANING. A CHARACTER MAY NOTICE THAT IT HAS BEEN USED ABOUT A DIFFERENT THING, AND ABOUT FOUR OF THEM DID.**
10. **THE RECORD ON THOSE BOARDS HAS STOOD NINETY-NINE DAYS AND NOT ONE PERSON IN THAT YARD HAS ASKED WHAT IT IS NINETY-NINE DAYS OF.** About nine people there can say that figure out loud this morning without looking at anything. **ROW NINETEEN IS AN ORIGIN AND REACHES ONE HUNDRED ON CHAPTER 901, WHICH IS NOT THIS BLOCK's, AND WHAT THE RECORD IS NINETY-NINE DAYS OF IS NOT ANSWERED HERE AND MAY NOT BE ANSWERED BY A FIGURE.**
11. **THE FIVE FIGURES A ROOM PAYS FOR AND THE MAN AT THE END OF THE SECOND TABLE.** He is not a puzzle, his fifth line is not to be paid, nobody answered him on the one hundred and eighty-first morning, and nobody named what he is owed.
12. **NELL CRAY's NINE WORDS ARE UNANSWERED AND WERE NOT HANDED ON IN THIS BLOCK**, and the question Adrian Vale put down out loud was not asked a second time and was not carried into this block as its opening question.
13. **FOUR DEBTS ARE UNPAID AND NONE OF THIS BLOCK'S TO PAY:** `state/volume-16-close.md`, `state/volume-16-roll-summary.md`, `state/volume-12-close.md` AND `state/volume-12-roll-summary.md` DO NOT EXIST, MEASURED BY LISTING `state/`.
14. **SIX ANSWERS NO PHASE MAY GIVE:** whether the founder's clause is valid, lawful, just, unjust or moral, and whether it works; what the common measure was; who proposed it; why the first public settlement was abandoned; the condition on the seat; and whether the question Adrian Vale put down will ever be the thing the tally pays for, and if it is not, then what is.

## WIDENED OR HARDENED IN THIS BLOCK, IN THE CHAPTERS AND NOT IN THIS DOCUMENT

1. **THE BACK PAGE TOOK ONE LINE IN TEN MORNINGS, AND IT TOOK IT BECAUSE SOMEBODY ASKED IN THAT YARD, AND NEITHER THE QUESTION NOR THE ANSWER WENT ON IT.** Thirty-six lines on Chapters 891 to 898 and thirty-seven from Chapter 899, **and the rule a successor will be tempted to write is not a rule, it is what happened once.** THIS IS THE MOST IMPORTANT LINE IN THE BLOCK AND IT IS THE ONE NOBODY WAS THANKED FOR.
2. **A REASON AND A NO CARRY DIFFERENT WEIGHTS IN THAT YARD AND THE YARD HAS NOT DECIDED WHICH ONE IT WANTS.** Two reasons were given out loud in ten mornings, on the one hundred and seventy-ninth and the one hundred and eighty-second, and the second one was about the yard and not about the lane. **IT IS NOT SETTLED THAT THE REASON WAS BETTER, AND IT IS NOT SETTLED THAT THE REFUSAL WAS CHEAPER, AND NEITHER IS PRINTED AS A FIGURE ANYWHERE.** A man who has given a reason in that yard is still a man nobody has thanked when the light goes.
3. **THE ASKERS HAVE OUTNUMBERED THE ANSWERERS ALL TEN MORNINGS, AND ON THE LAST MORNING OF THE BLOCK THE YARD REFUSED ONE QUESTION NINE TIMES WITHOUT ONE PERSON GIVING A REASON.** Six mornings out of ten put a question out loud in that yard. **WHETHER A YARD THAT SPENT FIFTY MORNINGS LEARNING TO PUT AN ASKING ON A PAGE CAN REFUSE A THING OUT LOUD NINE TIMES WITHOUT A REASON IS THE ONE THING THIS BLOCK DID NOT KNOW AT THE START AND STILL DOES NOT KNOW AT THE END.**
4. **THE BOY OF SEVENTEEN SAID A FIGURE OUT LOUD ON FOUR OF THESE TEN MORNINGS AND NO FIGURE ON THE OTHER SIX, AND HE HAS NEVER SAID WHAT THE NUMBER IS ABOUT.** It is not the morning number, it is not the count of mornings a man has been at the foot of that bank, and it slipped by one in the fifty chapters behind. **IT MAY NOT BE GIVEN AN INTERCEPT, A PROJECTION, A CARRIED FIGURE OR A CLOSING TOTAL.**
5. **THE COUNT OF COUNTED MONTHS IS SEVEN AND DID NOT MOVE ON ANY MORNING OF THIS BLOCK**, the figure on the sheet at that gatepost is four hundred and eleven, the toll is ninety-eight and spent, the bid was appended at four hundred and fifty days and not run, **and the second space on that form is empty and the space on the form that came down that lane is a different space and is empty as well.**
6. **THE CRAFT FIGURES A SUCCESSOR INHERITS FROM THIS BLOCK, MEASURED AND NOT INHERITED:** **27,965 WORDS AND 180 DIVIDERS** against a house band of 25,675 to 30,575 words and 125 to 183 dividers, neither measure harmonised to the other. **THE DUPLICATED-PARAGRAPH FIGURE IS FOUR INSTANCES IN TWO HUNDRED AND SEVENTY, 1.5 PER CENT, AGAINST TWENTY-ONE, 7.8 PER CENT, WHEN THE BLOCK LANDED AND 4.2 PER CENT ACROSS THE FIFTY CHAPTERS BEHIND** — and the two surviving repeats are both the figure block's lane line, at two instances of its negative wording and two of its positive one, **which is the architecture of this volume and not a prose defect, and a successor who harmonises that line into one wording across a block has destroyed the only figure that describes the scenes.**

# VOLUME 20, BLOCK 0004 — CHAPTERS 961 TO 970 — OPEN THREADS AT THE END OF THE LAST MORNING OF THE VOLUME

14. **A REVIEW PHASE FOUND FOUR MEASUREMENT CLAIMS IN `state/volume-20-batch-0004-summary.md` THAT DID NOT SURVIVE RE-MEASUREMENT. **THREE WERE WRONG AND HAVE BEEN CORRECTED AGAINST THE FILES, AND ONE WAS RIGHT AND HAD NO UNIT, WHICH IS A FAULT OF THE SAME KIND. **NO CHAPTER FILE WAS TOUCHED BY THE REPAIR, NOT ONE WORD OF PROSE MOVED, AND THE PLOT IS UNCHANGED; EVERY FIX BELOW IS IN A RECORD, A STATE FILE OR THE CLOSE's CONTRACT.**

    1. **FIVE STALE WORD ROWS IN SECTION 2, AND A TABLE THAT CONTRADICTED ITS OWN TOTAL.** 962 WAS PUBLISHED AT 3,006 AND IS 3,001; 965 AT 2,918 AND IS 2,917; 966 AT 3,041 AND IS 3,040; 967 AT 3,025 AND IS 3,030; 969 AT 3,033 AND IS 3,031. **THE FIVE ROWS SUMMED TO 29,875 WHILE THE BLOCK ROW SAID 29,871, AND THE BLOCK TOTAL AND ALL TEN DIVIDER ROWS WERE CORRECT THROUGHOUT. **FOUR OF THE FIVE ARE CHAPTERS THE VERIFICATION PASS EDITED AND THE FIFTH, 969, IS A CHAPTER IT DID NOT EDIT, SO ITS ROW WAS NEVER CORRECTLY MEASURED.** ALL TEN ROWS NOW SUM TO THE BLOCK ROW.
    2. **"NINE WORDS AND TWO CHAPTERS HIGHER" WAS WRONG ON THE COUNT AND ON THE DIRECTION.** THE REPAIR IS ON **FOUR** CHAPTERS AND ONE OF THEM WENT DOWN: **962 MINUS TWO, 965 PLUS TWO, 966 PLUS TWO, 967 PLUS SEVEN**, WHICH SUM TO NINE. 962 LOSING TWO WORDS OF PROSE AND ITS PUBLISHED ROW ALSO BEING TWO WORDS TOO HIGH IS A COINCIDENCE OF ARITHMETIC AND NOT A CANCELLATION.
    3. **THE END-OF-DAY BEAT'S "EIGHT OF TEN DOWN TO THREE OF TEN" WAS CORRECT AND HAD NO UNIT, WHICH IS WHY IT COULD NOT BE REPRODUCED.** THE UNIT IS **THE BEAT's OPENING SENTENCE, LOCATED BY ITS TEXT AND NOT BY ITS POSITION**, AND ON CHAPTER 970 THAT SENTENCE IS THE SECOND SENTENCE OF ITS PARAGRAPH AND NOT THE FIRST, WHICH IS WHY A POSITIONAL INSTRUMENT RETURNS SEVEN AND TWO. **THE PROSE-VARIETY COUNT CORROBORATES THE EIGHT INDEPENDENTLY: TWENTY-EIGHT PAIR-INSTANCES IS EXACTLY EIGHT FILES TAKEN TWO AT A TIME, AND THREE IS THREE FILES TAKEN TWO AT A TIME.** ALL THREE CONVENTIONS ARE NOW PUBLISHED — BY TEXT EIGHT DOWN TO THREE, WHOLE PARAGRAPH THREE DOWN TO NONE, FIRST SENTENCE BY POSITION SEVEN DOWN TO TWO. **A FIGURE WITH NO UNIT IS NOT A FALSIFIABLE CLAIM BUT A RUMOUR, AND THAT IS THE STANDING METHOD HERE.**
    4. **THE HEDGE FAMILY WAS PUBLISHED AS "AT **ZERO AND ZERO** ON ALL TEN MORNINGS NOW, AND THAT IS A MEASUREMENT" AND IT IS **THREE AND THREE** UNDER THE TWO GUARDS THE CONTRACT SPECIFIES.** THE THREE ARE ALL *AT ABOUT NINE THAT SECOND TABLE WAS CARRIED* ON `c = 34`, `c = 38` AND `c = 40`, WHERE *ABOUT NINE* IS THE HOUSE's OWN HOUR AND *THE SECOND TABLE* IS A REGISTER CARRIER. **THE PROSE IS CORRECT AND WAS NOT AND MUST NOT BE ALTERED TO SATISFY AN INSTRUMENT; THE INSTRUMENT WAS INCOMPLETE.** A THIRD GUARD, EXCLUDING AN ORDINAL *SECOND*, IS NOW WRITTEN INTO `workspace/volume-20/batch-0005/PROMPT.md` ITEM 6 AND MEASURED: **ZERO AND ZERO ON ALL FOUR BLOCKS OF VOLUME 20 AND ON VOLUME 19's FORTY, STILL CATCHING THE REPAIRED 967 BREACH WHOSE UNIT IS *MINUTES*, AND LEAVING VOLUME 17's FORTY REAL *IN ABOUT FOUR MINUTES* HEDGES FIRING UNTOUCHED — A GUARD THAT HIDES A REAL FAMILY IS A GUARD THAT HIDES THE NEXT BREACH AS WELL.** THE FALSE SENTENCE WAS CORRECTED IN PLACE RATHER THAN DELETED, BECAUSE A RECORD THAT DISAPPEARS ITS OWN ERROR CANNOT BE CHECKED AGAINST IT.**

    **AND A FIFTH STALE FIGURE WAS FOUND IN THE SAME PASS AND WAS NOT AMONG THE FOUR: **THE EXACT-DUPLICATE PARAGRAPH COUNT WAS PUBLISHED AS *TWO GROUPS IN THIRTEEN INSTANCES* AFTER THE REPAIR, AND IT IS **ONE GROUP IN TEN INSTANCES** NOW**, THE THIRTEEN BEING THE STATE BEFORE THE REPAIR. IT DROPPED BECAUSE THREE MORNINGS STOPPED REPEATING A PARAGRAPH AND NOT BECAUSE ANY FIGURE, CARRIER, CELL OR COUNT MOVED, **AND A FIGURE THAT MOVED WITH THE PROSE AND WAS NOT RE-PUBLISHED IS THE SAME DEFECT AS A FIGURE THAT WAS NEVER MEASURED.** **

    **WHAT THE CLOSE INHERITS FROM ALL FIVE: **A MEASUREMENT PUBLISHED BESIDE A REPAIR MUST BE RE-MEASURED AFTER THE REPAIR AND MUST CARRY ITS UNIT, ITS CONVENTION AND ITS BEFORE-AND-AFTER, OR IT IS NOT A MEASUREMENT. THREE OF THE FOUR BLOCKS BEHIND THE CLOSE PUBLISHED A PROSE-VARIETY FIGURE NO INSTRUMENT REPRODUCES ACROSS A BLOCK BOUNDARY, AND THIS BLOCK'S OWN INSTRUMENT REPRODUCES NONE EITHER, SO THE CLOSE MUST PUBLISH ONE FIGURE WITH ONE STATED CONVENTION AND SAY WHICH PUBLISHED FIGURES IT REPRODUCES AND WHICH IT DOES NOT.** **

## VOLUME 22, BLOCK 0001 — THE SECOND PASS, TWO CRAFT THREADS KEPT, TWO WITHDRAWN, THREE OPENED

81. **A readout is the one part of a chapter nobody inside the chapter ever sees, so it is where the fourth wall gets in without anybody looking for it.** `chapter-1018.md` printed *nothing in this volume or any other has opened it* in its bid sentence, and it stood through a first pass, a review, a commit and a second block written against it. What it would have corrupted is the prohibition on meta-language, which the rest of that block holds on ten of ten and which one sentence in one readout breaks silently, because every check that counts figures reads past the sentence and counts a carrier instead. **A readout may name a district, a basin, a run of mornings, a sheet, a wall, a boat, a gatepost or a piece of paper. It may not name the book it is printed in. Read every clause of a readout as a sentence a man in that yard could have said; one clause in every ten files of that block would not have survived that reading.**
82. **A figure's carrier is the point of view of the morning he is in, and he can walk into a morning told from inside somebody else if one sentence he would say is set next to a sentence about him.** `chapter-1013.md` is told from her and its second half ended on the turner, who is the point of view of `chapter-1011.md` and is nowhere in `chapter-1013.md`. What it would have corrupted is the figure that what she did and did not do in the minute after a figure went out four feet off her is the whole of that morning, and that is the morning the one promise of Volume 22 is begun on. The test is one line and it is not a figure: **name the person the morning is told from, then ask whether that person could have thought each sentence of it. A chapter may report what a person noticed about somebody else; it may not report somebody else's noticing as though it were the reader's.**

It recorded that the block record published **427** and **421** for a count the files give as **426** and **420**, and that an instrument stripping punctuation returns a fault of zero. **Withdrawn as state, not as fact: the disagreement it describes is repaired and published in `state/volume-22-batch-0001-summary.md` §6, the word has no cap and nobody sets one, and a thread whose content is a number about a word is not a thread about this book.** Nothing else in the tree depended on it.

It recorded that two rows of the block record's own repetition ledger — the figure-block family at `0 / 0 of 45 / 0` and the closing-ledger family at `2 / 2 of 45 / 2` — measured at **358 / 45 of 45 / 18** and **118 / 45 of 45 / 3**. **Both sets are withdrawn.** A claim about prose that the instrument making it cannot reproduce is not published again, and neither set was carried into a chapter. The underlying observation survives in thread 79 above and in the brief for block 0003, which names the ten readout sentences a writer may not inherit.
85. **THE PREMISE OF THE BOOK AND THE PREMISE OF THE LAST TWO VOLUMES ARE NOT THE SAME PREMISE, AND UNTIL THIS PASS NO FILE IN THIS TREE SAID SO.** `NOVEL_SPEC.md` states the book as *a practical auctioneer who auctions the rights to use objects that nobody can sell safely, in a system apocalypse*, male lead an auctioneer who assigns value to dangerous objects, genre system apocalypse. Counted case-insensitively over the chapter files: `auction` appears **17** times in Volume 01 and **0** times in Volume 21 and **0** times in Volume 22; `System` appears **7** times in Volume 01 and **0** in each of the two volumes behind this one; there are **no** System panels in either. It is not a repair any writer phase may make: restoring the auction room, the bidder, the sale or the System inside a volume whose contract forbids a new institution, a new enemy and a new system would be new plot.** What the writer phases may do, and what block 0003's brief now says in as many words, is keep the ground they are standing on and not invent a sale to make the book sound like its own logline. This is the class of thread 100 and thread 108: a run figure goes stale in a document exactly the way a ladder cell goes stale, and the fix is always to generate from the map and publish the result rather than to repair the document. **A WRITER OF THE NEXT FOUR BLOCKS SHOULD GENERATE THIS ROW RATHER THAN READ IT OFF A CARD, AND SHOULD PUBLISH ANY CELL THAT COMES OUT DIFFERENT FROM WHAT A CARD SAYS INSTEAD OF HARMONISING THE TWO.**
